{"parentUuid":"879a6b73-3ca3-4207-8999-792a41ce5fdc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c9d9748a-a4f2-49e1-b766-a1d746b7f0cd","agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SdwHHxSUM2GB2iQruWJ3Dd","type":"tool_result","content":"1:# Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — Design Spec (2026-07-07)\n2:\n3:**Status:** Approved by Phil (\"das ist echt cool\") — authoritative design source for the\n4:Trace Map redesign and Logs IA restructuring in the `enhanced-logging` epic.\n5:\n6:**Source artifacts (all in this directory):**\n7:- `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` — interactive, browser-verified design dummy\n8:  (open locally or at https://claude.ai/code/artifact/a4d6fd3a-7897-451c-8d52-6fabd30ede9b).\n9:  The dummy IS the spec for visual treatment, copy, and interaction feel; this document\n10:  adds the contract-level details and implementation constraints.\n11:- Screenshots: `tracemap-initial.png`, `tracemap-outage.png`, `tracemap-v2-capture.png`,\n12:  `tracemap-v2-banner.png`, `tracemap-v3-resized-errorfilter.png`, `warroom-active.png`.\n13:\n14:**Why (Phil's verdict on the shipped UX):** the flat bubble list and the disclosure tree\n15:(`trace-map-hierarchy`) are \"unbrauchbar\" as a monitoring surface. The Trace Map becomes a\n16:control-room (\"Leitstand\") health map: at a glance, see *where* it is red.\n17:\n18:---\n19:\n20:## 1. Information architecture\n21:\n22:Sidebar gets collapsible groups (covered by existing story `admin-sidebar-nesting`):\n23:\n24:```\n25:Logs                 (disclosure group, aria-expanded)\n26:├── Live Stream      /admin/logs            (existing page, unchanged)\n27:├── Trace Map        /admin/logs/trace-map  (NEW page — this spec)\n28:└── War Room         /admin/logs/war-room   (story: war-room-dedicated-page)\n29:Configuration        (disclosure group)\n30:├── General          /admin/config\n31:├── Role Mapping     /admin/config/role-mapping\n32:└── Claim Mapping    /admin/config/claim-mapping\n33:```\n34:\n35:The Trace Map is no longer a filter widget embedded in the logs page — it is its own\n36:LiveView. The flat `TraceMapBubble` strip and the `TraceMapTree` disclosure tree are\n37:retired from the logs page in favor of this view (Live Stream keeps its own simpler\n38:filters; exact migration is a story-scoping decision).\n39:\n40:## 2. The radial map\n41:\n42:**Hierarchy:** center `nebu` → domain circles → endpoint satellites.\n43:Domains come from the existing heuristic `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1`\n44:(`/_matrix/client`, `/_matrix/media`, `/admin`, `/health`, catch-all) — REUSE it, do not\n45:duplicate. Satellites are the observed `route_template`s, first-observed order.\n46:\n47:**Geometry (from the dummy, SVG viewBox −560 −400 1120 800):** center r=52 at origin;\n48:domains r=38 on a ring R1≈195 at fixed angles (−48°, 48°, 132°, −132° for 4 domains —\n49:distribute evenly if domain count differs); satellites r=24 on R2≈128 around their domain,\n50:fanned over `min(130°, n·26°)` centered on the domain's outward angle. Edges are trimmed\n51:lines (node edge + 4px), `--line` color, opacity ~.55.\n52:\n53:**Status model:** per node, a sliding **60 s** window over its log events.\n54:`error > 0 → error`, else `warn > 0 → warn`, else `ok`. A parent's status is the worst\n55:of its children (satellite → domain → nebu). Nodes heal automatically when the window\n56:passes — no manual acknowledge.\n57:\n58:**Visual states (Obsidian theme tokens from `server/assets/tailwind.config.js`):**\n59:\n60:| State | Fill | Stroke | Extras |\n61:|---|---|---|---|\n62:| ok | `base-200` @ 35% opacity | `success` @ 55%, 1.5px | label dimmed — healthy recedes |\n63:| warn | `#431407` (warn-bg) | `warning`, 2.5px + soft glow | ▲ badge with warn count |\n64:| error | `#450a0a` (err-bg) | `error`, 3px + glow + pulse animation | ✕ badge with error count |\n65:| selected | — | extra `primary` ring at r+7, 2.5px | orange = selection ONLY |\n66:| logging off | `base-200` @ 15% | `text-disabled`, 1.5px **dashed** | count shows \"off\" |\n67:| temp capture | as status | status color, **dashed** | countdown mm:ss on the node |\n68:\n69:Status colors are reserved for health, primary orange for selection — never cross-used.\n70:Status is never conveyed by color alone: badges (▲/✕ + count), \"off\" text, aria-labels.\n71:Counters inside nodes (requests/60 s; center shows `N/min`) in mono font. Event \"pings\"\n72:(expanding rings) on nodes are decorative and disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`\n73:(as is the error pulse).\n74:\n75:## 3. Interactions\n76:\n77:- **Click / Enter / Space on a node** toggles it as a stream filter (multi-select, OR\n78:  within the dimension; domain matches all its satellites). Click on `nebu` clears all\n79:  filters. Active filters render as removable chips in the stream header + \"Clear all\".\n80:- **Hover / focus** shows a tooltip (name, route, status, req/warn/err per 60 s, CTA).\n81:  Tooltip hides on click. Focus also targets the node in the inspector.\n82:- **Inspector panel (right column)** for the focused/clicked node — see §4.\n83:- **Legend card** (right column) explains all six states; always visible.\n84:\n85:## 4. Inspector = per-node logging control panel\n86:\n87:Live stats (requests / warnings / errors per 60 s, status pill, last 5 events) PLUS\n88:logging controls:\n89:\n90:- **Min level** select (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR) — wires to the per-endpoint collection\n91:  level from `admin-logs-retention-settings` (done). On a domain node or `nebu` the\n92:  setting cascades to all descendant endpoints, with the note \"Applies to all endpoints\n93:  in this domain\" / \"on the map\".\n94:- **Retention** select (1 h / 24 h / 7 d / 30 d) — same config backend.\n95:- **Capture control** for long-polling endpoints (§5): state line + Enable/Disable button\n96:  with live countdown.\n97:\n98:UI contract: the panel is BUILT once per inspected node and only its stat values update\n99:per tick — selects must never lose value/focus to a re-render (same class of bug as the\n100:trace-map-filter-reset incident).\n101:\n102:## 5. Long-polling endpoints: off by default, temporary capture\n103:\n104:Supersedes the UI concept of `noise-endpoint-bucketing` (ready-for-dev — realign that\n105:story): instead of a \"noise bucket\", low-signal long-polling endpoints (default list:\n106:`/_matrix/client/v3/sync`, `/admin/keepalive`, `/health`; config-driven) are\n107:**logging-off by default**:\n108:\n109:- Rendered gray + dashed, count \"off\", no log data collected, no status.\n110:- **Click enables temporary capture for 5 minutes** (announce via aria-live): node comes\n111:  alive with dashed status stroke + countdown on the node and in the inspector;\n112:  \"Disable now\" in the inspector ends it early. After expiry it falls back to off\n113:  automatically — server-side timer, no client state.\n114:- While capturing, a second click toggles the stream filter as normal.\n115:\n116:## 6. War-room visibility\n117:\n118:While a war-room session is active (from `war-room-dedicated-page` / existing\n119:`war_room_manager.ex`): a red banner across the top of the Trace Map view — recording\n120:dot + \"War Room session active — capturing {user} · {elapsed}\" + \"Open War Room\" button.\n121:(Nav-item dot and topbar pill belong to the war-room story; the banner belongs to the\n122:Trace Map story.)\n123:\n124:## 7. Live stream panel (bottom of the Trace Map view)\n125:\n126:- Compact mono log lines: time, level (colored), route, message. Autoscroll pauses on\n127:  hover. Filtered by the map selection AND the level filter.\n128:- **Resizable:** drag handle between map and stream (`role=\"separator\"`,\n129:  `aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"`, tabindex=0; pointer drag with capture, ArrowUp/Down\n130:  ±32px, dblclick resets to default 218px, Home/End max/min; clamp ~64px…viewport−140px).\n131:- **Level filter pills** (DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR, `aria-pressed`, level colors) with the\n132:  leitstand shortcut: when ALL levels are active, clicking one shows ONLY that level;\n133:  clicking the sole active level restores all; otherwise plain toggle.\n134:\n135:## 8. Hard implementation constraints\n136:\n137:1. **Filter/selection/expansion state lives in LiveView assigns driven by URL query\n138:   params** (`push_patch` + `handle_params/3`) — NEVER client-DOM-only state on nodes\n139:   that re-render under live traffic (root cause of the trace-map-filter-reset bug).\n140:   Bookmarkable URLs fall out for free.\n141:2. **CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no inline JS.** Client-only behavior (tooltip\n142:   positioning, resizer drag, ping animations) goes through the established\n143:   `app.js` `wire*` / `JS.dispatch` pattern or a `phx-hook` defined in\n144:   `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`.\n145:3. SVG is rendered server-side in HEEx (positions computed in Elixir — plain module,\n146:   ADR-018/021 compliant); LiveView patches update node classes/counters.\n147:4. Accessibility is blocking, not advisory: nodes are focusable\n148:   `role=\"button\" aria-pressed` elements with status+counts in `aria-label`;\n149:   `aria-live=\"polite\"` announcements for new errors (throttled) and capture\n150:   enable/disable; visible focus rings; WCAG AA contrast per the Obsidian tokens;\n151:   Playwright tests use `getByRole`/`getByLabel`.\n152:5. No sensitive data on the map, in tooltips, or in the stream (No-Sensitive-Data law —\n153:   route templates and counts only, never payloads/tokens/message content).\n154:6. E2E coverage must grow with every UI story (pipeline CI gate).\n155:\n156:## 9. Suggested story cut\n157:\n158:1. `admin-sidebar-nesting` (exists, ready-for-dev) — matches the dummy as-is.\n159:2. `war-room-dedicated-page` (exists, backlog) — add ACs: nav-item recording dot,\n160:   topbar recording pill with elapsed time.\n161:3. **NEW `trace-map-leitstand`** — the radial map page: hierarchy, status model +\n162:   propagation, visual states, node filters + chips, tooltip, legend, war-room banner,\n163:   resizable stream + level filter. (Largest story; may split map vs. stream panel.)\n164:4. **NEW `trace-map-node-controls`** — inspector logging controls (level/retention wiring,\n165:   cascade) + long-polling off-by-default with temporary capture (realigns\n166:   `noise-endpoint-bucketing`).\n167:\n168:## Addendum (2026-07-07, Phil)\n169:- §1 IA correction: the Logs group child pointing at /admin/logs/config is labeled\n170:  **\"Log Retention\"** (not \"Configuration\"; navkey `logs-config` stays). Resolves UX\n171:  advisory A1 (duplicate accessible name vs. the top-level Configuration group).\n172:\n173:## Addendum (2026-07-07, design owner — §5 correction)\n174:- \"Logging off\" suppresses DEBUG/INFO/WARN collection and status, but ERROR entries are\n175:  ALWAYS persisted and always drive node status (red dashed stroke + ✕ badge on an off\n176:  node).              <div\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-logging\"\n                class=\"mt-3 border-t border-base-300 pt-3\"\n              >\n                <h3 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Logging</h3>\n                <form\n                  id={\"tm-insp-logging-form-\" <> node_dom_key(@inspected_node.key)}\n                  data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-logging-form\"\n                  phx-change=\"configure_logging\"\n                  class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\"\n                >\n                  <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"node_key\" value={@inspected_node.key} />\n                  <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                    <label\n                      for={\"tm-insp-level-\" <> node_dom_key(@inspected_node.key)}\n                      class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n                    >\n                      Min level\n                    </label>\n                    <select\n                      id={\"tm-insp-level-\" <> node_dom_key(@inspected_node.key)}\n                      name=\"min_level\"\n                      data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-level-select\"\n                      class=\"select select-bordered select-sm\"\n                    >\n                      <option\n                        :for={level <- @ui_levels}\n                        value={level}\n                        selected={level == @inspected_node.min_level}\n                      >\n                        {level}\n                      </option>\n                    </select>\n                  </div>\n                  <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n                    <label\n                      for={\"tm-insp-retention-\" <> node_dom_key(@inspected_node.key)}\n                      class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n                    >\n                      Retention\n                    </label>\n                    <select\n                      id={\"tm-insp-retention-\" <> node_dom_key(@inspected_node.key)}\n                      name=\"retention_days\"\n                      data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-retention-select\"\n                      class=\"select select-bordered select-sm\"\n                    >\n                      <option\n                        :for={{value, text} <- retention_options()}\n                        value={value}\n                        selected={value == @inspected_node.retention_days}\n                      >\n                        {text}\n                      </option>\n                    </select>\n                  </div>\n                </form>\n                <p\n                  data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-scope-note\"\n                  class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1\"\n                >\n                  {@inspected_node.scope_note}\n                </p>\n\n                <%!-- AC5/AC6/AC9: capture controls — only for off-by-default\n                      long-polling endpoints (any inspected key satisfying\n                      off_endpoint?/1, regardless of its domain/satellite\n                      display kind — /health is simultaneously both). --%>\n                <div\n                  :if={@inspected_node.off_tier}\n                  data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture\"\n                  class=\"mt-2 border-t border-base-300 pt-2\"\n                >\n                  <div\n                    data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture-state\"\n                    class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary flex items-center gap-2\"\n                  >\n                    <%= if @inspected_node.capture do %>\n                      Capture active ·\n                      <span\n                        data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture-countdown\"\n                        data-expires-at={capture_expires_at_unix(@inspected_node.capture)}\n                      >{capture_countdown_text(@inspected_node.capture, @now_ms)}</span>\n                    <% else %>\n                      Logging off (long-polling)\n                    <% end %>\n                  </div>\n                  <%!-- AC9: the accessible name STARTS WITH the visible\n                        button text (\"Disable now\" / \"Enable for 5 min\") and\n                        then names the endpoint — WCAG 2.5.3 Label-in-Name\n                        compliant (an aria-label that silently REPLACES the\n                        visible text with unrelated wording breaks voice\n                        control AND `getByRole(name:)`'s substring match on\n                        the visible label; this format satisfies both). --%>\n                  <button\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-capture-toggle\"\n                    phx-click={if @inspected_node.capture, do: \"disable_capture\", else: \"enable_capture\"}\n                    phx-value-key={@inspected_node.key}\n                    aria-label={\n                      if @inspected_node.capture,\n                        do: \"Disable now — capture for \" <> @inspected_node.route,\n                        else: \"Enable for 5 min — capture for \" <> @inspected_node.route\n                    }\n                    class=\"btn btn-sm btn-outline mt-1 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary focus-visible:outline-offset-2\"\n                  >\n                    {if @inspected_node.capture, do: \"Disable now\", else: \"Enable for 5 min\"}\n                  </button>\n                  <p class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mt-1\">\n                    Long-polling endpoints stay off by default and fall back automatically.\n                  </p>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Legend</h2>\n            <div\n              :for={{state, dot_class, text} <- legend_rows()}\n              data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"\n              data-legend-state={state}\n              class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n            >\n              <span class={[\"tm-legend-dot\", dot_class]} aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n              <span>{text}</span>\n            </div>\n            <p class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mt-2\">\n              Domains inherit the worst status of their satellites; nebu inherits the worst domain.\n              Nodes heal automatically once the 60 s window passes.\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n      </div>\n\n      <%!-- AC10: throttled aria-live announcer for new errors (endpoint name\n            + no message content — spec §8). --%>\n      <div data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\" class=\"sr-only\">\n        {@announcement}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  # ── One SVG map node (nebu / domain / satellite) ──────────────────────────\n  #\n  # Focusable `role=\"button\"` per AC10; Enter/Space activation is wired by\n  # the TraceMapLeitstand hook (SVG <g> has no native keyboard activation).\n  # `aria-pressed` reflects selection; nebu is never a toggle target (always\n  # \"false\" — clicking it CLEARS filters instead).\n  attr(:node, :map, required: true)\n\n  defp map_node(assigns) do\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <g\n      id={@node.dom_id}\n      data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"\n      data-node-key={@node.key}\n      data-node-kind={@node.kind}\n      data-node-status={@node.status}\n      data-node-off={to_string(@node.off_tier)}\n      data-node-selected={to_string(@node.selected)}\n      role=\"button\"\n      tabindex=\"0\"\n      aria-pressed={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && to_string(@node.pressed)}\n      aria-label={@node.aria_label}\n      data-tooltip-name={@node.label}\n      data-tooltip-route={@node.route}\n      data-tooltip-status={@node.tooltip_status}\n      data-tooltip-counts={@node.tooltip_counts}\n      data-tooltip-cta={@node.tooltip_cta}\n      transform={\"translate(#{@node.x} #{@node.y})\"}\n      phx-click={@node.click_event}\n      phx-value-key={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && @node.key}\n      class={\"tm-node tm-node--\" <> @node.status}\n    >\n      <circle class=\"tm-selring\" r={@node.r + 7} />\n      <circle data-testid=\"trace-map-node-core\" class=\"tm-node-core\" r={@node.r} />\n      <%= case @node.kind do %>\n        <% \"nebu\" -> %>","numLines":180,"startLine":1020,"totalLines":1744}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"042c2eda-2402-4ee4-bfd9-8864ea28e575","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"3ed107fd-3dd1-4369-8c76-395627fe96a9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c9d9748a-a4f2-49e1-b766-a1d746b7f0cd","agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ND3jt5fCNnWivnkhbMEvSX","type":"tool_result","content":"1199\t        <% \"nebu\" -> %>\n1200\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-2\">nebu</text>\n1201\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"16\">{@node.counts.requests}/min</text>\n1202\t        <% \"domain\" -> %>\n1203\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-1\">{@node.label}</text>\n1204\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"14\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n1205\t        <% _satellite -> %>\n1206\t          <%!-- AC4/AC6/AC8: an off-tier (quiescent-or-erroring) satellite\n1207\t                shows \"off\" text, never a number (spec: \"count shows 'off'\n1208\t                text\"); a capturing (temp) satellite shows the countdown\n1209\t                instead of the plain count (AC6 — mm:ss, data-expires-at for\n1210\t                the TraceMapLeitstand hook's client tick, no server tick\n1211\t                needed); everything else shows the plain numeric count. --%>\n1212\t          <%= cond do %>\n1213\t            <% @node.off_tier and is_nil(@node.capture) -> %>\n1214\t              <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">off</text>\n1215\t            <% @node.status == \"temp\" -> %>\n1216\t              <text\n1217\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-node-countdown\"\n1218\t                data-expires-at={@node.countdown_expires_at}\n1219\t                class=\"tm-ncount tm-countdown\"\n1220\t                y=\"4\"\n1221\t              >{@node.countdown_text}</text>\n1222\t            <% true -> %>\n1223\t              <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n1224\t          <% end %>\n1225\t          <text class=\"tm-nlabel\" y={@node.r + 15}>{@node.label}</text>\n1226\t      <% end %>\n1227\t      <g\n1228\t        :if={@node.status == \"warn\"}\n1229\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"\n1230\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--warn\"\n1231\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n1232\t      >\n1233\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n1234\t        <text y=\"3.5\">▲{badge_count(@node.counts.warnings)}</text>\n1235\t      </g>\n1236\t      <g\n1237\t        :if={@node.status == \"error\"}\n1238\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"\n1239\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--error\"\n1240\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n1241\t      >\n1242\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n1243\t        <text y=\"3.5\">✕{badge_count(@node.counts.errors)}</text>\n1244\t      </g>\n1245\t    </g>\n1246\t    \"\"\"\n1247\t  end\n1248\t\n1249\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1250\t  # View-model builders\n1251\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1252\t\n1253\t  # Groups observed endpoints by domain (REUSING TraceMapTree's canonical\n1254\t  # heuristic), merges in the always-present off-list endpoints (AC4), then\n1255\t  # appends URL-selected domains no traffic has produced a satellite for yet.\n1256\t  defp domain_groups(endpoint_order, extra_domains) do\n1257\t    groups =\n1258\t      endpoint_order\n1259\t      |> TraceMapTree.group_endpoints_by_domain()\n1260\t      |> merge_off_endpoints(@long_polling_endpoints)\n1261\t\n1262\t    known = Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, _leaves} -> domain end)\n1263\t\n1264\t    groups ++ (extra_domains |> Enum.reject(&(&1 in known)) |> Enum.map(&{&1, []}))\n1265\t  end\n1266\t\n1267\t  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4): the off-list endpoints\n1268\t  # are ALWAYS on the map, appended to their domain's existing leaves (or as\n1269\t  # a brand-new domain group, for a domain with no other observed traffic\n1270\t  # yet) — deliberately AFTER any REAL-traffic groups/leaves, and never\n1271\t  # duplicating a leaf already present (a broadcast for the off-list route\n1272\t  # itself, e.g. the geometry test's own `/health` traffic). This ordering\n1273\t  # preserves the pre-existing, shipped trace-map-leitstand geometry\n1274\t  # contract: `TraceMapGeometry.domain_angles/1`'s fixed spec angles apply\n1275\t  # only on an EXACT domain-key-list match against the canonical 4 — as\n1276\t  # long as real traffic already produces that exact order, appending\n1277\t  # off-list leaves/domains AFTER it (via List.update_at/2, never\n1278\t  # reordering) never disturbs that match.\n1279\t  defp merge_off_endpoints(groups, off_endpoints) do\n1280\t    Enum.reduce(off_endpoints, groups, fn off_route, acc ->\n1281\t      domain = TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(off_route)\n1282\t\n1283\t      case Enum.find_index(acc, fn {d, _leaves} -> d == domain end) do\n1284\t        nil ->\n1285\t          acc ++ [{domain, [off_route]}]\n1286\t\n1287\t        idx ->\n1288\t          List.update_at(acc, idx, fn {d, leaves} ->\n1289\t            if off_route in leaves, do: {d, leaves}, else: {d, leaves ++ [off_route]}\n1290\t          end)\n1291\t      end\n1292\t    end)\n1293\t  end\n1294\t\n1295\t  # DOM order (AC10 natural Tab order): nebu first, then each domain followed\n1296\t  # by its own satellites.\n1297\t  defp build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns) do\n1298\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n1299\t    states = assigns.node_states\n1300\t    selected = assigns.selected\n1301\t    captures = assigns.captures\n1302\t\n1303\t    satellite_statuses =\n1304\t      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n1305\t        {key, TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, key), now)}\n1306\t      end)\n1307\t\n1308\t    # AC4/AC5/AC6/AC8: the DISPLAYED status per satellite — \"temp\" while\n1309\t    # capturing, \"off\"/\"error\" for a quiescent/erroring off-tier satellite\n1310\t    # (errors-always-persist amendment), else the plain raw status. This is\n1311\t    # an OVERLAY on top of satellite_statuses: propagation math (below)\n1312\t    # keeps using the RAW ok/warn/error rank, only the rendered attribute\n1313\t    # and badge/count/aria-label logic ever see the display string.\n1314\t    satellite_display =\n1315\t      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n1316\t        {key, display_status(key, Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, key), captures)}\n1317\t      end)\n1318\t\n1319\t    domain_statuses =\n1320\t      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n1321\t        child_statuses =        <% \"nebu\" -> %>\n          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-2\">nebu</text>\n          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"16\">{@node.counts.requests}/min</text>\n        <% \"domain\" -> %>\n          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-1\">{@node.label}</text>\n          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"14\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n        <% _satellite -> %>\n          <%!-- AC4/AC6/AC8: an off-tier (quiescent-or-erroring) satellite\n                shows \"off\" text, never a number (spec: \"count shows 'off'\n                text\"); a capturing (temp) satellite shows the countdown\n                instead of the plain count (AC6 — mm:ss, data-expires-at for\n                the TraceMapLeitstand hook's client tick, no server tick\n                needed); everything else shows the plain numeric count. --%>\n          <%= cond do %>\n            <% @node.off_tier and is_nil(@node.capture) -> %>\n              <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">off</text>\n            <% @node.status == \"temp\" -> %>\n              <text\n                data-testid=\"trace-map-node-countdown\"\n                data-expires-at={@node.countdown_expires_at}\n                class=\"tm-ncount tm-countdown\"\n                y=\"4\"\n              >{@node.countdown_text}</text>\n            <% true -> %>\n              <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n          <% end %>\n          <text class=\"tm-nlabel\" y={@node.r + 15}>{@node.label}</text>\n      <% end %>\n      <g\n        :if={@node.status == \"warn\"}\n        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"\n        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--warn\"\n        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n      >\n        <circle r=\"9\" />\n        <text y=\"3.5\">▲{badge_count(@node.counts.warnings)}</text>\n      </g>\n      <g\n        :if={@node.status == \"error\"}\n        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"\n        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--error\"\n        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n      >\n        <circle r=\"9\" />\n        <text y=\"3.5\">✕{badge_count(@node.counts.errors)}</text>\n      </g>\n    </g>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # View-model builders\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Groups observed endpoints by domain (REUSING TraceMapTree's canonical\n  # heuristic), merges in the always-present off-list endpoints (AC4), then\n  # appends URL-selected domains no traffic has produced a satellite for yet.\n  defp domain_groups(endpoint_order, extra_domains) do\n    groups =\n      endpoint_order\n      |> TraceMapTree.group_endpoints_by_domain()\n      |> merge_off_endpoints(@long_polling_endpoints)\n\n    known = Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, _leaves} -> domain end)\n\n    groups ++ (extra_domains |> Enum.reject(&(&1 in known)) |> Enum.map(&{&1, []}))\n  end\n\n  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4): the off-list endpoints\n  # are ALWAYS on the map, appended to their domain's existing leaves (or as\n  # a brand-new domain group, for a domain with no other observed traffic\n  # yet) — deliberately AFTER any REAL-traffic groups/leaves, and never\n  # duplicating a leaf already present (a broadcast for the off-list route\n  # itself, e.g. the geometry test's own `/health` traffic). This ordering\n  # preserves the pre-existing, shipped trace-map-leitstand geometry\n  # contract: `TraceMapGeometry.domain_angles/1`'s fixed spec angles apply\n  # only on an EXACT domain-key-list match against the canonical 4 — as\n  # long as real traffic already produces that exact order, appending\n  # off-list leaves/domains AFTER it (via List.update_at/2, never\n  # reordering) never disturbs that match.\n  defp merge_off_endpoints(groups, off_endpoints) do\n    Enum.reduce(off_endpoints, groups, fn off_route, acc ->\n      domain = TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(off_route)\n\n      case Enum.find_index(acc, fn {d, _leaves} -> d == domain end) do\n        nil ->\n          acc ++ [{domain, [off_route]}]\n\n        idx ->\n          List.update_at(acc, idx, fn {d, leaves} ->\n            if off_route in leaves, do: {d, leaves}, else: {d, leaves ++ [off_route]}\n          end)\n      end\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # DOM order (AC10 natural Tab order): nebu first, then each domain followed\n  # by its own satellites.\n  defp build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns) do\n    now = assigns.now_ms\n    states = assigns.node_states\n    selected = assigns.selected\n    captures = assigns.captures\n\n    satellite_statuses =\n      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n        {key, TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, key), now)}\n      end)\n\n    # AC4/AC5/AC6/AC8: the DISPLAYED status per satellite — \"temp\" while\n    # capturing, \"off\"/\"error\" for a quiescent/erroring off-tier satellite\n    # (errors-always-persist amendment), else the plain raw status. This is\n    # an OVERLAY on top of satellite_statuses: propagation math (below)\n    # keeps using the RAW ok/warn/error rank, only the rendered attribute\n    # and badge/count/aria-label logic ever see the display string.\n    satellite_display =\n      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n        {key, display_status(key, Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, key), captures)}\n      end)\n\n    domain_statuses =\n      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        child_statuses = Enum.map(leaves, &Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, &1))\n        own = TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, domain), now)\n        {domain, TraceMapStatus.worst([own | child_statuses])}\n      end)\n\n    # Known Challenge 1: a domain whose satellites are ALL currently\n    # displayed \"off\" is itself \"off\" (gray/dashed, no status) — derived\n    # from the CHILDREN'S DISPLAY strings (not the off-list membership of\n    # the domain key itself), so a domain with a capturing or erroring\n    # off-tier child correctly falls through to the normal worst-child\n    # rendering instead.\n    domain_display =\n      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        child_display = Enum.map(leaves, &Map.fetch!(satellite_display, &1))\n\n        display =\n          if leaves != [] and Enum.all?(child_display, &(&1 == \"off\")) do\n            \"off\"\n          else\n            Atom.to_string(Map.fetch!(domain_statuses, domain))\n          end\n\n        {domain, display}\n      end)\n\n    nebu_status =\n      TraceMapStatus.worst([\n        TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, \"nebu\"), now)\n        | Map.values(domain_statuses)\n      ])\n\n    nebu_node =\n      build_node(\n        \"nebu\",\n        \"nebu\",\n        layout.nebu,\n        nebu_status,\n        Atom.to_string(nebu_status),\n        \"nebu\",\n        \"all traffic\",\n        selected,\n        states,\n        now,\n        captures\n      )\n\n    domain_and_satellite_nodes =\n      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        domain_node =\n          build_node(\n            domain,\n            \"domain\",\n            Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain),\n            Map.fetch!(domain_statuses, domain),\n            Map.fetch!(domain_display, domain),\n            domain_label(domain),\n            domain,\n            selected,\n            states,\n            now,\n            captures\n          )\n\n        satellite_nodes =\n          Enum.map(leaves, fn route ->\n            build_node(\n              route,\n              \"satellite\",\n              Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route),\n              Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, route),\n              Map.fetch!(satellite_display, route),\n              satellite_label(route),\n              route,\n              selected,\n              states,\n              now,\n              captures\n            )\n          end)\n\n        [domain_node | satellite_nodes]\n      end)\n\n    [nebu_node | domain_and_satellite_nodes]\n  end\n\n  # AC4/AC5/AC6/AC8: overlays the off-by-default/temp-capture states on top\n  # of a satellite's raw worst-status rank. ERROR always wins (the\n  # errors-always-persist amendment) — an off-tier satellite with a\n  # windowed error renders \"error\" (with data-node-off still true, so the\n  # dashed off-tier stroke stays — see map_node/1), not the healthy-looking\n  # \"off\" state.\n  defp display_status(key, raw_status, captures) do\n    cond do\n      Map.has_key?(captures, key) -> \"temp\"\n      off_endpoint?(key) and raw_status == :error -> \"error\"\n      off_endpoint?(key) -> \"off\"\n      true -> Atom.to_string(raw_status)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp build_node(key, kind, position, raw_status, display_status, label, route, selected, states, now, captures) do\n    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n    off_tier = kind == \"satellite\" and off_endpoint?(key)\n    capture = if kind == \"satellite\", do: Map.get(captures, key), else: nil\n\n    %{\n      key: key,\n      kind: kind,\n      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n      #\n      # KIND-prefixed (dev cycle 2): \"/health\" is simultaneously a domain key\n      # AND a satellite key (a single-segment route is its own domain per\n      # TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1) — a key-only id produced two\n      # elements with the SAME id, which corrupts LiveView's id-keyed DOM\n      # patching for exactly those nodes (observed live: the toggle patch for\n      # the /health domain never applied).\n      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> kind <> \"-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n      x: coord(position.x),\n      y: coord(position.y),\n      r: position.r,\n      status: display_status,\n      off_tier: off_tier,\n      capture: capture,\n      countdown_text: if(capture, do: capture_countdown_text(capture, now), else: nil),\n      countdown_expires_at: if(capture, do: capture_expires_at_unix(capture), else: nil),\n      selected: is_selected,\n      pressed: is_selected,\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      counts: counts,\n      aria_label: node_aria_label(kind, label, route, display_status, raw_status, counts, capture, now),\n      tooltip_status: status_word(raw_status),\n      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),","numLines":260,"startLine":1199,"totalLines":1744}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3ed107fd-3dd1-4369-8c76-395627fe96a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"e7d6ae0d-7419-4205-8580-567e326ea243","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c9d9748a-a4f2-49e1-b766-a1d746b7f0cd","agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JTC5ivW6QVLoMhEzGzoXWw","type":"tool_result","content":"1458\t      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n1459\t      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n1460\t      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n1461\t    }\n1462\t  end\n1463\t\n1464\t  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n1465\t\n1466\t  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n1467\t    domain_edges =\n1468\t      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n1469\t        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n1470\t      end)\n1471\t\n1472\t    satellite_edges =\n1473\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n1474\t        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n1475\t        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n1476\t      end)\n1477\t\n1478\t    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n1479\t  end\n1480\t\n1481\t  defp edge(a, b) do\n1482\t    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n1483\t    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n1484\t  end\n1485\t\n1486\t  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n1487\t\n1488\t  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n1489\t    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n1490\t      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n1491\t        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n1492\t    end)\n1493\t  end\n1494\t\n1495\t  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n1496\t  # satellites' lines.\n1497\t  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n1498\t\n1499\t  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n1500\t    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n1501\t  end\n1502\t\n1503\t  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n1504\t\n1505\t  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n1506\t    key = assigns.inspected\n1507\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n1508\t    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n1509\t\n1510\t    {kind, label, route} =\n1511\t      cond do\n1512\t        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n1513\t        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {\"domain\", domain_label(key), key}\n1514\t        true -> {\"satellite\", satellite_label(key), key}\n1515\t      end\n1516\t\n1517\t    %{\n1518\t      key: key,\n1519\t      kind: kind,\n1520\t      label: label,\n1521\t      route: route,\n1522\t      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n1523\t      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n1524\t      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, []),\n1525\t      # AC1/AC2/AC3: the \"Logging\" panel — built once per inspected node.\n1526\t      # min_level/retention_days are pure functions of (key, stored\n1527\t      # server_config), never of @now_ms/counts, so a stat-tick re-render\n1528\t      # produces byte-identical select markup (AC3).\n1529\t      scope_note: scope_note(kind),\n1530\t      min_level: current_min_level(key),\n1531\t      retention_days: current_retention_days(key),\n1532\t      # AC5/AC6/AC9: capture controls apply to ANY off-list key regardless\n1533\t      # of its domain/satellite display kind (/health is simultaneously\n1534\t      # both — see TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1's moduledoc).\n1535\t      off_tier: off_endpoint?(key),\n1536\t      capture: Map.get(assigns.captures, key)\n1537\t    }\n1538\t  end\n1539\t\n1540\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1541\t  # Labels / text helpers\n1542\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1543\t\n1544\t  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n1545\t\n1546\t  defp satellite_label(route) do\n1547\t    route\n1548\t    |> String.split(\"/\")\n1549\t    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n1550\t    |> List.last()\n1551\t    |> case do\n1552\t      nil -> route\n1553\t      segment -> segment\n1554\t    end\n1555\t  end\n1556\t\n1557\t  # AC1/AC2: the inspector's scope note (dummy copy).\n1558\t  defp scope_note(\"satellite\"), do: \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n1559\t  defp scope_note(\"domain\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n1560\t  defp scope_note(\"nebu\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints on the map\"\n1561\t\n1562\t  # AC1/AC2: pre-population from server_config (Dev Notes key format:\n1563\t  # log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>). DB-free guard\n1564\t  # mirrors the rest of this module's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) contract —\n1565\t  # sensible defaults (INFO / 7 d) when unset or DB-free.\n1566\t  defp current_min_level(key) do\n1567\t    read_config_value(\"log_min_level_\" <> key, \"info\") |> to_string() |> String.upcase()\n1568\t  end\n1569\t\n1570\t  defp current_retention_days(key) do\n1571\t    read_config_value(\"log_retention_days_\" <> key, \"7\") |> to_string()\n1572\t  end\n1573\t\n1574\t  defp read_config_value(key, default) do\n1575\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n1576\t      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n1577\t      Map.get(config, key, default)\n1578\t    else\n1579\t      default\n1580\t    end\n1581\t  end\n1582\t\n1583\t  # AC2 (story amendment 2026-07-07 / test-review M1): 1 d / 7 d / 30 d —\n1584\t  # the dummy's \"1 h\" option is dropped, day-granularity only.\n1585\t  defp retention_options, do: @retention_options\n1586\t\n1587\t  # A stable id-safe fragment for the inspected node's <label for=...>/\n1588\t  # <select id=...> pairing (AC9) — reuses the same Base64 scheme as each\n1589\t  # SVG node's own dom_id.\n1590\t  defp node_dom_key(key), do: Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false)\n1591\t\n1592\t  defp capture_expires_at_unix(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}) do\n1593\t    Integer.to_string(DateTime.to_unix(expires_at))\n1594\t  end\n1595\t\n1596\t  defp capture_countdown_text(capture, now_ms) do\n1597\t    now = DateTime.from_unix!(div(now_ms, 1000))\n1598\t    format_clock(TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(capture, now))\n1599\t  end\n1600\t\n1601\t  # off\") rather than the generic status word (status is never color-alone);\n1606\t  # \"temp\" names the capture state + remaining time.\n1607\t  defp node_aria_label(kind, label, route, display_status, raw_status, counts, capture, now_ms) do\n1608\t    name =\n1609\t      case kind do\n1610\t        \"nebu\" -> \"nebu\"\n1611\t        \"domain\" -> label <> \" domain\"\n1612\t        _ -> label <> \" endpoint\"\n1613\t      end\n1614\t\n1615\t    base = Enum.join([name, route], \" · \")\n1616\t\n1617\t    case display_status do"}]},"uuid":"4aa371c2-f760-45e6-98f7-e01d60555f34","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:37:04.416Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex","content":"      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n    }\n  end\n\n  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n\n  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n    domain_edges =\n      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n      end)\n\n    satellite_edges =\n      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n      end)\n\n    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n  end\n\n  defp edge(a, b) do\n    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n  end\n\n  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n\n  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n  # satellites' lines.\n  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n\n  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n  end\n\n  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n\n  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n    key = assigns.inspected\n    now = assigns.now_ms\n    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n\n    {kind, label, route} =\n      cond do\n        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {\"domain\", domain_label(key), key}\n        true -> {\"satellite\", satellite_label(key), key}\n      end\n\n    %{\n      key: key,\n      kind: kind,\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, []),\n      # AC1/AC2/AC3: the \"Logging\" panel — built once per inspected node.\n      # min_level/retention_days are pure functions of (key, stored\n      # server_config), never of @now_ms/counts, so a stat-tick re-render\n      # produces byte-identical select markup (AC3).\n      scope_note: scope_note(kind),\n      min_level: current_min_level(key),\n      retention_days: current_retention_days(key),\n      # AC5/AC6/AC9: capture controls apply to ANY off-list key regardless\n      # of its domain/satellite display kind (/health is simultaneously\n      # both — see TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1's moduledoc).\n      off_tier: off_endpoint?(key),\n      capture: Map.get(assigns.captures, key)\n    }\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Labels / text helpers\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n\n  defp satellite_label(route) do\n    route\n    |> String.split(\"/\")\n    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n    |> List.last()\n    |> case do\n      nil -> route\n      segment -> segment\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC1/AC2: the inspector's scope note (dummy copy).\n  defp scope_note(\"satellite\"), do: \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n  defp scope_note(\"domain\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n  defp scope_note(\"nebu\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints on the map\"\n\n  # AC1/AC2: pre-population from server_config (Dev Notes key format:\n  # log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>). DB-free guard\n  # mirrors the rest of this module's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) contract —\n  # sensible defaults (INFO / 7 d) when unset or DB-free.\n  defp current_min_level(key) do\n    read_config_value(\"log_min_level_\" <> key, \"info\") |> to_string() |> String.upcase()\n  end\n\n  defp current_retention_days(key) do\n    read_config_value(\"log_retention_days_\" <> key, \"7\") |> to_string()\n  end\n\n  defp read_config_value(key, default) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n      Map.get(config, key, default)\n    else\n      default\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC2 (story amendment 2026-07-07 / test-review M1): 1 d / 7 d / 30 d —\n  # the dummy's \"1 h\" option is dropped, day-granularity only.\n  defp retention_options, do: @retention_options\n\n  # A stable id-safe fragment for the inspected node's <label for=...>/\n  # <select id=...> pairing (AC9) — reuses the same Base64 scheme as each\n  # SVG node's own dom_id.\n  defp node_dom_key(key), do: Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false)\n\n  defp capture_expires_at_unix(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}) do\n    Integer.to_string(DateTime.to_unix(expires_at))\n  end\n\n  defp capture_countdown_text(capture, now_ms) do\n    now = DateTime.from_unix!(div(now_ms, 1000))\n    format_clock(TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(capture, now))\n  end\n\n  # /* base-200 */\n  fill-opacity: 0.35;\n  stroke: #22c55e; /* success */\n  stroke-opacity: 0.55;\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n}\n\n/* warn: amber stroke + soft glow + ▲ badge (badge markup is server-side) */\n.tm-node--warn .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #431407; /* status-warn-bg */\n  fill-opacity: 0.9;\n  stroke: #f59e0b; /* warning */\n  stroke-width: 2.5;\n  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.45));\n}\n\n/* error: red stroke + glow + pulse (pulse disabled under reduced motion) */\n.tm-node--error .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #450a0a; /* status-error-bg */\n  fill-opacity: 0.95;\n  stroke: #ef4444; /* error */\n  stroke-width: 3;\n  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.6));\n  animation: tm-errpulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;\n}\n\n@keyframes tm-errpulse {\n  0%,\n  100% {\n    stroke-opacity: 1;\n    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 10px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.6));\n  }\n  50% {\n    stroke-opacity: 0.5;\n    filter: drop-shadow(0 0 3px rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.25));\n  }\n}\n\n/* Node labels/counters (mono counters per spec §2) */\n.tm-nname {\n  font-family: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #f9fafb;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-nlabel {\n  font-family: Inter, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #9ca3af;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-ncount {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 11.5px;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n  fill: #9ca3af;\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n/* ok recedes: dimmed label (status is never color-alone — AC4) */\n.tm-node--ok .tm-nlabel {\n  opacity: 0.75;\n}\n\n.tm-node--warn .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--error .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--warn .tm-ncount,\n.tm-node--error .tm-ncount {\n  fill: #f9fafb;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n/* enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4/AC5/AC6/AC8): long-polling\n * endpoints — off by default (gray, dashed, \"off\" text), one click enables\n * a dashed temporary capture. The errors-always-persist amendment (an\n * off-tier node with a windowed error) stays on the normal\n * `.tm-node--error` red/pulse rule EXCEPT it must keep the dashed stroke\n * and drop the pulse — selected via the `[data-node-off=\"true\"]` co-marker\n * (never a duplicated status string) so \"off\" and \"erroring\" compose. */\n.tm-node--off .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #1f2937; /* base-200 */\n  fill-opacity: 0.15;\n  stroke: #6b7280; /* text-disabled */\n  stroke-opacity: 0.8;\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n  stroke-dasharray: 4 4;\n  filter: none;\n  animation: none;\n}\n\n.tm-node--off .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--off .tm-ncount {\n  fill: #6b7280;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n.tm-node--temp .tm-node-core {\n  stroke-dasharray: 6 3;\n}\n\n.tm-node--temp .tm-countdown {\n  fill: #9ca3af;\n}\n\n/* AC4 amendment (2026-07-07): \"off silences noise, never failures\" — an\n * off-tier node with a windowed error stays dashed (never the solid\n * pulse), communicating that it is STILL off-tier even while erroring. */\n.tm-node--error[data-node-off=\"true\"] .tm-node-core {\n  stroke-dasharray: 4 4;\n  animation: none;\n}\n\n/* Selection ring — primary orange ONLY, never used for health (AC4) */\n.tm-selring {\n  fill: none;\n  stroke: #f97316; /* primary */\n  stroke-width: 2.5;\n  display: none;\n}\n\n.tm-node[data-node-selected=\"true\"] .tm-selring {\n  display: block;\n}\n\n/* AC10: visible focus ring on every node (never removed) */\n[data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]:focus-visible {\n  outline: 2px solid #f97316;\n  outline-offset: 2px;\n}\n\n/* Status badges (▲/✕ + count) — status is never conveyed by color alone */\n.tm-badge {\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.tm-badge circle {\n  stroke: #0d1117; /* base-400 map background */\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n}\n\n.tm-badge--warn circle {\n  fill: #f59e0b;\n}\n\n.tm-badge--error circle {\n  fill: #ef4444;\n}\n\n.tm-badge text {\n  font-family: \"JetBrains Mono\", ui-monospace, monospace;\n  font-size: 9.5px; /* UX gate A7: match the dummy's badge size */\n  font-weight: 700;\n  fill: #1c1917;\n  text-anchor: middle;\n}\n\n/* UX gate V2: white on #ef4444 is only 3.76:1 — the theme's own\n * error-content token (#000000, 5.58:1 on the error red) is used instead\n * (the dummy's white-on-red badge text has the same flaw; AC10's 4.5:1\n * hard gate overrides the dummy here). */\n.tm-badge--error text {\n  fill: #000000;\n}\n\n/* Decorative event pings (created by the TraceMapLeitstand hook on status\n * transitions; the animation itself is pure CSS — AC11) */\n.tm-ping {\n  fill: none;","numLines":200,"startLine":40,"totalLines":429}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c81627e4-2477-4565-9a47-ee0595d8e63c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"8171bf72-8faa-430c-abb4-9ebb6439d9cd","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CcpHgHAS7efQ5ujcAjqUZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018JjRdPBQoct1Ty3gZxXcFc","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\ndef lin(c):\n    c = c/255\n    return c/12.92 if c <= 0.03928 else ((c+0.055)/1.055)**2.4\n\ndef lum(rgb):\n    r,g,b = rgb\n    return 0.2126*lin(r)+0.7152*lin(g)+0.0722*lin(b)\n\ndef contrast(c1, c2):\n    l1, l2 = lum(c1), lum(c2)\n    lighter, darker = max(l1,l2), min(l1,l2)\n    return (lighter+0.05)/(darker+0.05)\n\noff_text = (0x6b,0x72,0x80)\nmap_bg = (0x0d,0x11,0x17)\nblended_bg = (16,21,28)  # approx blend of base-200@15% over base-400\ntext_disabled_real = (0x4b,0x55,0x63)\ntext_secondary = (0x9c,0xa3,0xaf)\n\nprint(\"off-text(#6b7280) vs map bg(#0d1117):\", contrast(off_text, map_bg))\nprint(\"off-text(#6b7280) vs blended bg:\", contrast(off_text, blended_bg))\nprint(\"real text-disabled(#4b5563) vs map bg:\", contrast(text_disabled_real, map_bg))\nprint(\"text-secondary(#9ca3af) vs map bg:\", contrast(text_secondary, map_bg))\nEOF\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1511,"cache_read_input_tokens":64732,"output_tokens":1160,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1511},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1160,"cache_read_input_tokens":64732,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1511,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1511,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpHgFK4c2jREkEWUjYwP","attributionAgent":"nebu-ux-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"0aef2221-b1a0-4730-83ba-47294e773af1","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:38:51.499Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"97f13f7a-5842-4eaa-93cb-e0d56345058e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CcpHhJsQB1TrWPQDYsYUm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Si2T1m11XsfATHx71pmc1M","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python3 - <<'EOF'\ndef lin(c):\n    c = c/255\n    return c/12.92 if c <= 0.03928 else ((c+0.055)/1.055)**2.4\n\ndef lum(rgb):\n    r,g,b = rgb\n    return 0.2126*lin(r)+0.7152*lin(g)+0.0722*lin(b)\n\ndef contrast(c1, c2):\n    l1, l2 = lum(c1), lum(c2)\n    lighter, darker = max(l1,l2), min(l1,l2)\n    return (lighter+0.05)/(darker+0.05)\n\ndef blend(fg, bg, alpha):\n    return tuple(fg[i]*alpha + bg[i]*(1-alpha) for i in range(3))\n\nstroke = (0x6b,0x72,0x80)\nbg = (0x0d,0x11,0x17)\nblended_stroke = blend(stroke, bg, 0.8)\nprint(\"blended stroke:\", blended_stroke)\nprint(\"stroke contrast vs bg:\", contrast(blended_stroke, bg))\nEOF\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1277,"cache_read_input_tokens":66243,"output_tokens":902,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1277},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":902,"cache_read_input_tokens":66243,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1277,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1277,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpHhHwLuJhkKDzhq8UHC","attributionAgent":"nebu-ux-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"21ddec23-5297-4a29-9c9f-ea9c7aad8d19","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:39:03.239Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

This is the SAME\n  # backend concept the superseded `noise-endpoint-bucketing` story would\n  # have used — `long_polling_endpoints/0` is the single source of truth,\n  # reused by `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2` (server-side\n  # enforcement) and available for future stories (war-room-dedicated-page's\n  # \"safe scope\" option, trace-map-hierarchy's domain-grouping).\n  @long_polling_endpoints [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/admin/keepalive\", \"/health\"]\n\n  @retention_options [{\"1\", \"1 d\"}, {\"7\", \"7 d\"}, {\"30\", \"30 d\"}]\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  The config-driven off-by-default long-polling endpoint list (AC4/AC13).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec long_polling_endpoints() :: [String.t()]\n  def long_polling_endpoints, do: @long_polling_endpoints\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Whether `route` is one of the off-by-default long-polling endpoints\n  (AC4). `nil`/non-binary routes are never off-tier.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec off_endpoint?(term()) :: boolean()\n  def off_endpoint?(route) when is_binary(route), do: route in @long_polling_endpoints\n  def off_endpoint?(_route), do: false\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Pure computation of which OBSERVED endpoint route templates (from\n  `endpoint_order`) a config change on `node_key` applies to (AC1/AC2):\n\n    * a satellite key (anything that is not its own domain, and not\n      `\"nebu\"`) cascades only to itself (\"Applies to this endpoint only\") —\n      returned regardless of whether it is actually present in\n      `endpoint_order` yet.\n    * a domain key (`TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` maps it to\n      itself) cascades to every endpoint in `endpoint_order` whose domain\n      is that key — this correctly includes the domain key itself when it","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"47ffe2e5-bfb2-49d9-9ef9-fcfd53748166","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:39:47.280Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n  @base_path \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n\n  # UI level-pill vocabulary (spec §7) <-> LogStore level strings. {"parentUuid":"826df93d-e0af-4a06-998b-9749d01fd9f7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c9d9748a-a4f2-49e1-b766-a1d746b7f0cd","agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013BoCDUjrZFJaPhujz4tdft","type":"tool_result","content":"    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n    schedule_tick()\n\n    # AC5/AC6/Known Challenge 2: the captures table is the timer — every\n    # tick re-reads it (DB-free guarded) so an expiry is discovered by a\n    # fresh `now()` comparison, never by a process-owned countdown. A key\n    # that drops out of the fresh read (and was not already removed by an\n    # explicit disable_capture/2, which updates @captures itself) has\n    # auto-expired — announce it (AC5's \"no manual intervention\").\n    previous_keys = socket.assigns.captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new()\n    fresh_captures = load_active_captures()\n    expired_keys = MapSet.difference(previous_keys, fresh_captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new())\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      |> assign(:captures, fresh_captures)\n      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n\n    socket =\n      case Enum.to_list(expired_keys) do\n        [] -> socket\n        [key | _rest] -> assign(socket, :announcement, \"Temporary capture expired for #{key}\")\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, socket}\n  end\n\n  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n  # session starts later.\n  #\n  # UX gate V1: the banner itself is not a live region (it contains a\n  # per-second clock) — a session START/STOP transition is announced as a\n  # one-shot state-change sentence through the sr-only announcer instead.\n  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"24bb1f06-b647-4474-ab63-4c9dd8ec55c7","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:39:53.341Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n    schedule_tick()\n\n    # AC5/AC6/Known Challenge 2: the captures table is the timer — every\n    # tick re-reads it (DB-free guarded) so an expiry is discovered by a\n    # fresh `now()` comparison, never by a process-owned countdown. A key\n    # that drops out of the fresh read (and was not already removed by an\n    # explicit disable_capture/2, which updates @captures itself) has\n    # auto-expired — announce it (AC5's \"no manual intervention\").\n    previous_keys = socket.assigns.captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new()\n    fresh_captures = load_active_captures()\n    expired_keys = MapSet.difference(previous_keys, fresh_captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new())\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      |> assign(:captures, fresh_captures)\n      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n\n    socket =\n      case Enum.to_list(expired_keys) do\n        [] -> socket\n        [key | _rest] -> assign(socket, :announcement, \"Temporary capture expired for #{key}\")\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, socket}\n  end\n\n  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n  # session starts later.\n  #\n  # UX gate V1: the banner itself is not a live region (it contains a\n  # per-second clock) — a session START/STOP transition is announced as a\n  # one-shot state-change sentence through the sr-only announcer instead.\n  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"826df93d-e0af-4a06-998b-9749d01fd9f7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

the\n718\t  # 60-second window judges seeded rows by their REAL age, not by mount time.\n719\t  defp entry_timestamp(entry) do\n720\t    case entry[:inserted_at] do\n721\t      %DateTime{} = dt  defp normalize_route(route) when is_binary(route), do: route\n  defp normalize_route(route), do: to_string(route)\n\n  defp ui_level(\"debug\"), do: \"DEBUG\"\n  defp ui_level(\"warning\"), do: \"WARN\"\n  defp ui_level(\"error\"), do: \"ERROR\"\n  defp ui_level(_), do: \"INFO\"\n\n  defp truncate(nil), do: \"\"\n\n  defp truncate(message) when is_binary(message) do\n    if String.length(message) > 120, do: String.slice(message, 0, 120) <> \"…\", else: message\n  end\n\n  defp truncate(message), do: truncate(to_string(message))\n\n  # Live broadcasts carry no timestamp -> \"now\"; mount-time LogStore history\n  # rows carry `inserted_at` (DateTime/NaiveDateTime) -> converted so the\n  # 60-second window judges seeded rows by their REAL age, not by mount time.\n  defp entry_timestamp(entry) do\n    case entry[:inserted_at] do\n      %DateTime{} = dt ->\n        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n\n      %NaiveDateTime{} = naive ->\n        dt = DateTime.from_naive!(naive, \"Etc/UTC\")\n        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n\n      _ ->\n        now = DateTime.utc_now()\n        {DateTime.to_unix(now, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(now, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Render\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def render(assigns) do\n    groups = domain_groups(assigns.endpoint_order, assigns.extra_domains)\n\n    layout =\n      TraceMapGeometry.layout(\n        Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} -> %{key: domain, satellites: leaves} end)\n      )\n\n    assigns =\n      assigns\n      |> assign(:nodes, build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns))\n      |> assign(:edges, build_edges(groups, layout))\n      |> assign(:visible_lines, visible_lines(assigns))\n      |> assign(:inspected_node, inspected_node(assigns))\n      |> assign(:ui_levels, @ui_levels)\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <div\n      id=\"trace-map-leitstand\"\n      phx-hook=\"TraceMapLeitstand\"\n      class=\"flex flex-col gap-3\"\n      style=\"height: calc(100vh - 6.5rem);\"\n    >\n      <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between shrink-0\">\n        <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">Trace Map</h1>\n      </div>\n\n      <%!-- AC1/AC11 Scenario B: cascade success message (\"Updated N endpoints\").\n            A dedicated assign (not Phoenix's built-in @flash — this LiveView's\n            shared admin layout does not render @flash today) so both the\n            LiveView test's `render(view) =~ regex` and the E2E getByText A window property (not a DOM attribute) so\n      // LiveView's DOM patching can never sweep it away.\n      window.__nebuTraceMapHookReady = true;\n\n      // ── AC10: Enter/Space activates the focused SVG node ──────────────\n      this.onNodeKeydown = function (event) {\n        if (event.key !== \"Enter\" && event.key !== \" \" && event.key !== \"Spacebar\") return;\n        var node = event.target.closest\n          ? event.target.closest('[data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"]')\n          : null;\n        if (!node) return;\n        event.preventDefault();\n        traceMapHideTooltip(self.el);\n        node.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent(\"click\", { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));\n      };\n      this.el.addEventListener(\"keydown\", this.onNodeKeydown);\n\n      // ── AC7: tooltip on hover + focus ──────────────────────────────────","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b7bce78e-33ba-48bd-9e64-9723ad9d3ee1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
