{"parentUuid":"29b58260-4036-44c6-9b3d-a1827fcf1507","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c9d9748a-a4f2-49e1-b766-a1d746b7f0cd","agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CgZ1jpUtvpY7nnhB9rCK2J","type":"tool_result","content":" * (never a duplicated status string) so \"off\" and \"erroring\" compose. */\n.tm-node--off .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #1f2937; /* base-200 */\n  fill-opacity: 0.15;\n  stroke: #6b7280; /* text-disabled */\n  stroke-opacity: 0.8;\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n  stroke-dasharray: 4 4;\n  filter: none;\n  animation: none;\n}\n\n.tm-node--off .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--off .tm-ncount {\n  fill: #6b7280;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n---\n.tm-legend-dot--off {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1.5px dashed #4b5563;\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"75a6e5a5-9315-422d-9fcb-e118d0156406","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:44:02.826Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":" * (never a duplicated status string) so \"off\" and \"erroring\" compose. */\n.tm-node--off .tm-node-core {\n  fill: #1f2937; /* base-200 */\n  fill-opacity: 0.15;\n  stroke: #6b7280; /* text-disabled */\n  stroke-opacity: 0.8;\n  stroke-width: 1.5;\n  stroke-dasharray: 4 4;\n  filter: none;\n  animation: none;\n}\n\n.tm-node--off .tm-nlabel,\n.tm-node--off .tm-ncount {\n  fill: #6b7280;\n  opacity: 1;\n}\n\n---\n.tm-legend-dot--off {\n  background: transparent;\n  border: 1.5px dashed #4b5563;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"29b58260-4036-44c6-9b3d-a1827fcf1507","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"75a6e5a5-9315-422d-9fcb-e118d0156406","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1c34e564315c2089","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CcpJ67cNveg6j9iGhx387","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## 3 violations, 4 advisories\n\nI read the full story (`docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md`, including both Story Amendments sections), the design spec + addenda (`_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md` §4/§5 + the two addenda), and the staged diff of `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex`, `server/assets/css/app.css`, and `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`. The stack is running (`docker compose ps`) but `/admin/logs/trace-map` sits behind OIDC login (302), so verification was done via static code + computed contrast math (WCAG relative-luminance formula) rather than an interactive click-through — flagging this as a limitation, not a pass.\n\n### Violations (blocking)\n\n**V1 — `.tm-node--off` text fails WCAG 1.4.3 (contrast) — reintroduces the class of bug already fixed once in this epic**\n`server/assets/css/app.css:151-166` (`.tm-node--off .tm-node-core { stroke: #6b7280 }`, `.tm-node--off .tm-nlabel, .tm-node--off .tm-ncount { fill: #6b7280 }`)\nComputed contrast of `#6b7280` against the map background (`#0d1117`, blended with the `.tm-node--off` fill at 15% opacity) is **~3.8–3.9:1**, below the 4.5:1 required for normal-size text (the \"off\" label is 11.5–12px). Per your prompt's explicit question: this is genuinely a violation, not a correctly-exempted \"disabled control\" case — the off-node is a fully **operable** element (`role=\"button\" tabindex=\"0\"`, clickable/Enter/Space-activatable to enable capture, per AC5). WCAG's \"inactive UI component\" exemption only applies to controls that are actually inert; this one is the opposite (it's *inviting* a click). The design spec table (`trace-map-leitstand-spec.md:66`) itself prescribes \"text-disabled\" for this state, but that predates the prior gate's fixed convention (\"use `text-secondary`, not `text-disabled`, for operable content\" — MEMORY/BOND lesson from the `trace-map-leitstand` V1 fix) and should not override it; spec §8.4 states accessibility is \"blocking, not advisory.\" Fix: swap to `text-secondary` (`#9ca3af`, computed **7.45:1**), matching the fixed convention already applied elsewhere in this same file (e.g. `.tm-node--temp .tm-countdown { fill: #9ca3af }`).\n\n**V2 — `.tm-node--off` stroke falls just under WCAG 1.4.11 non-text contrast (3:1)**\n`server/assets/css/app.css:151-160` — with `stroke-opacity: 0.8`, the effective stroke color blends to ≈2.93:1 against the map background, just under the 3:1 minimum for the boundary of a UI component (the node is a focusable control, not decoration). Fixing V1 by switching to `text-secondary` at full opacity clears this too (7.45:1 stroke), so both can be resolved with one change.\n\n**V3 — the off+error composite state's `aria-label` drops the \"off-tier\" information that's only conveyed visually**\n`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex:1413-1420` (`display_status/3`) sets `display_status = \"error\"` for an off-tier endpoint with a windowed error (per the amendment). But `node_aria_label/8` (`trace_map.ex:1607-1635`) only special-cases `\"off\"` and `\"temp\"` — the `\"error\"` value falls through to the generic `_ ->` branch, producing e.g. `\"health endpoint · /health · errors · 1 error · 0 warnings · 0 requests in the last 60 seconds\"`, identical to a normal always-on endpoint in error. The dashed-vs-solid stroke (`data-node-off=\"true\"` + `.tm-node--error[data-node-off=\"true\"]`, `app.css:179-182`) is the *only* signal that this endpoint is still off-by-default (DEBUG/INFO/WARN suppressed, only the error surfaced) — that distinction is real and functionally meaningful (per the amendment's own rationale: \"off silences noise, never failures\") but is invisible to AT users. This is exactly the \"status is never conveyed by color/shape alone\" principle from spec §2/§8.4, applied to the off/on distinction rather than health status. The tooltip can't compensate — it's `aria-hidden=\"true\"` and duplicates the node's own label (`trace_map.ex:847-848`), so the aria-label is the sole accessible channel. Fix: add a branch, e.g. `\"logging off · errors detected · \" <> pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"`, keyed off `off_tier` when `display_status == \"error\"`.\n\n### Advisories (non-blocking)\n\n**A1 — misleading token comment in the CSS**\n`server/assets/css/app.css:154` labels `#6b7280` as `/* text-disabled */`, but the real `text-disabled` Tailwind token is `#4b5563` (`server/assets/tailwind.config.js:47`); `#6b7280` is actually the DaisyUI `info` token. Harmless today, but could mislead a future maintainer into believing this already matches the previously-fixed convention. Worth correcting the comment when V1 is fixed.\n\n**A2 — two different `<label>` idioms for conceptually-identical \"retention\" controls**\nThe new inspector (`trace_map.ex:1033-1075`) uses explicit `<label for=\"...\">` / `<select id=\"...\">` pairing (correctly satisfying AC9's literal requirement). The existing `admin-logs-retention-settings` page (`server/lib/nebu/admin/templates/logs_config/index.html.heex:122-178`) instead wraps inputs in `<label class=\"form-control\">` + belt-and-suspenders `aria-label`. Both are WCAG-valid, but it's a second labelling pattern for the same conceptual control type in the same app — worth consolidating in a later cleanup, not blocking here.\n\n**A3 — dual countdown-tick write paths (server diff + client hook) on the same text node every second**\n`trace_map.ex:473-495` re-renders `capture_countdown_text/2` on the server's own 1s tick (LiveView diff), while `app.js:1484-1497` (`traceMapTickCountdowns`) independently recomputes the same mm:ss text from `data-expires-at` on its own 1s client interval. This is explicitly documented as intentional (\"visual smoothing\" vs. the DB-backed authoritative expiry — `app.js:1339-1348`) and is **not** the V1-class live-region bug (the countdown span is not inside the `aria-live` announcer, which only updates on discrete enable/disable/expiry events — confirmed at `trace_map.ex:1157-1159` vs. `trace_map.ex:430/442/494`). Still, worth a manual QA pass to confirm the two writers never visibly fight/flicker by ±1s.\n\n**A4 — legend dot reuses the real `text-disabled` token, but safely**\n`server/assets/css/app.css:392-395` (`.tm-legend-dot--off { border: 1.5px dashed #4b5563 }`) uses the actual `text-disabled` color, which would fail contrast as text — but the dot is `aria-hidden=\"true\"` (`trace_map.ex:1144`) and paired with adjacent explanatory text (`trace_map.ex:1145`), so no violation. Flagging only so it isn't mistaken for a validated \"off-state gray\" to copy elsewhere for actual text.\n\n### Things verified as compliant (no issues found)\n- Focus-preservation contract (AC3): `min_level`/`retention_days` select markup is a pure function of `(node key, server_config)` — never of `@now_ms`/`captures` — so per-tick re-renders produce byte-identical HTML and LiveView's diffing never touches the `<select>` DOM node (`trace_map.ex:1525-1531`, `1566-1572`). The capture toggle button's markup is likewise stable while capture state is unchanged.\n- Label-in-Name (WCAG 2.5.3): the capture button's `aria-label` (`\"Disable now — capture for …\"` / `\"Enable for 5 min — capture for …\"`) starts with the exact visible button text (`trace_map.ex:1107-1127`) — correctly fixed live per the dev's own comment.\n- Keyboard operability: Enter/Space activation for off/temp SVG nodes reuses the pre-existing generic `onNodeKeydown` handler (`app.js:1214-1226`), unchanged and correctly generic across all node states.\n- Countdown is not color-alone: the mm:ss text satisfies AC6/AC8's \"never color alone\" requirement.\n- \"Flex-beats-hidden\" trap: the new capture UI uses HEEx `:if` (full add/remove), not a `[hidden]` + `flex` CSS combination — the only place
